My sleep’s been pretty messed up. For a while I was only getting like two or three hours and still felt wired, like I didn’t need more. But lately it’s different — I’ll wake up before dawn feeling sick with worry, or I’ll sleep half the day and still feel exhausted.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed yourself moving or speaking more slowly than usual?\n\nPatient: Yeah. People at work mentioned I seemed slowed down, and I could feel it too. Even simple stuff, like putting a blood pressure cuff away or answering someone’s question, was taking me way longer than it normally would.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced any strange thoughts or heard voices that others couldn't hear?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I have. At work I’d hear my name over the clinic speaker, or like a monitor beeping for me, and I’d go check on it. But then nobody else heard it, and there wasn’t a patient calling or anything. It made me feel like I was losing track of what was real.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any thoughts that you think are not your own or that are not based in reality?\n\nPatient: Yeah. I got really convinced that I’d been chosen to fix how the whole clinic was taking care of patients. Like it felt like it was my responsibility, not just an idea.\n\nAnd even when my supervisor told me I was just an aide in training, I still thought they were trying to stop me or block what I was supposed to do. Looking back, it sounds strange, but at the time it felt completely real.\n\nDoctor: Have you had periods of feeling really down like this before?\n\nPatient: Yeah. After that high-energy period ended, I kind of crashed, and the down part has been going on for about nine months. Mostly feeling depressed, guilty, and just exhausted.\n\nDoctor: Have you had periods of feeling really down like this before the nine months that just passed?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure. I don’t remember having anything this bad before. Maybe I had normal low days, but not a long period like this that I can clearly point to.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period of significant depression like this before in your life?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think so. Not like this. The depressed, guilty, exhausted feeling has been going on for about nine months since I crashed after that high-energy period. Before that, I don’t really remember a period this significant.\n\nDoctor: Have you had more than one period of feeling depressed like this in your life?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think it’s been more than one. This depressed, guilty, exhausted part has just been this one long stretch, about nine months since I crashed after that high-energy period.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period in your life where you felt extremely energetic or irritable and didn't need much sleep?\n\nPatient: Yeah. There were days where I felt amazing, like I could do anything, almost unstoppable. But I’d also get really irritated fast—like if someone corrected my documentation or told me to slow down, I’d snap at them like they were insulting me.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods of depression like this before the nine months that just passed?\n\nPatient: No, not before this.
